    "english": "Virtue taken in by the realm of desire represents a type of deed which is both white and black, and which also ripens into something both white and black. Since its basic nature is free of affliction it is white but--since within a single mental stream it is also mixed with afflicted things--it is black as well. It ripens into something white because it gives forth a pleasant result but--since within the particular mental stream this is also mixed with suffering--we can also say it ripens into something black."

It is no contradiction though to say that, in this situation where the beings are looking this way, they are not objects such that they block other things from entering into the space they occupy. This is true for the following reason. A central mountain of the world which is square in shape, and a central mountain of the world which is round in shape, and the like, are objects such that they block other things from entering the space they occupy. Nevertheless, it is possible for both these things to occupy the space taken up by a single central mountain of the world. A red-hot ball of steel is something that's hot, but consider what happens when a person touches it after he has used the mantra of steel on his hand. The sensation that he feels is not a sensation of heat; on the contrary, it is a sensation of something not heat. ===== tibetan/319/report.md =====
# C05:319 — independent Tibetan-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3a (through 312); no future through-318 acceptance claimed.
Challenge nyon mongs pa → afflicted things first: a complete substantival rendering is proposed, with full master evidence and local mixed-with grammar. The negative nature statement is a whole phrase; outer causal scope stays unbanked.
Counts: 40 spans; 0 nulls; 28 non-null d5 word pairs (28 total d5); 0 d7 members.
Canonical generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5675 bytes. Exact native output is native/generator-v1/stdout.bin.
Canonical resolver EXIT=0; every intended source/English occurrence range matches resolved-ranges.json. Both rendered full fields equal the original source.

Full master objects and exact counts: ../evidence/full-lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-full-lexical-evidence.json. Comparanda remain reference only.
Every complete-field and leading-phrase parallel query returns this row alone; all-course results and C16/P1/TCS scope are preserved in ../evidence/.
Errata: 0. All five category screens with all five refutation grounds are recorded in errata-audit.json; uncertain suspicions are not filed.

All alignments remain provisional machine proposals; mechanical passes do not assert final semantic approval.

===== english/319/report.md =====
# C05:319 — English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3af9cbb47599bb40286637fc9e683b7cdf (through 312); future landing baseline was not supplied or claimed.
Challenge the scope of the four pas causal assignments first: each has its own complete clause and measured English occurrence; phyir is deliberately omitted.
The pleasant-result and afflicted/suffering mixtures remain separate. The repeated compounds retain two meaningful members each; supplied both and connective expansions stay outside.
Coverage: 40 spans; 25 non-null d5; 25 total d5; 4 d7; 0 nulls; 0 new errata.
All source-specific decisions, exact ranges and explicit omissions: decisions.json. Full source/context, material corpus/master objects and original physical bytes: ../evidence/.
Five-ground category screens: errata-audit.json. Exact-field searches each return this row alone; broader context queries are recorded with full rows and original corpus objects. No publication independence is asserted.
Canonical generator and resolver each ran once, both exit 0 and empty stderr. Full native streams, argv and hashes are under native-*-v1/.
EXIT=0; body bytes=5697; body SHA256=d000f3e81d34193dc7db4b8c4fd9b2bed9aecd05b92befa37b5a8682b0a1e1ed.
Spec SHA256=71939407820f1a1a75638e4efdb9a5cb8dcccbc8eb716fb300b4fa273085e0f6. Canonical ranges match all intended occurrences; rendered complete source strings equal the originals.
Mechanical checks are not semantic approval. Machine matches remain provisional and are never promoted into hgm_gloss.
One evidence-collection assumption failed before lexical queries: the verse ACIP is line-wrapped in its physical file. The failed method is preserved; corrected proof uses reversible Latin-1 whitespace folding and a complete raw extent.

===== reconciled/319/report.md =====
# C05:319 — independent reconciliation
Producer: Codex. Source/governance origin 49b66f3a through 312; a later through-318 acceptance baseline was not supplied or consumed.
Two dkar gnag parents and four unique color members replace the flattened color choices. The two extra Tibetan-only spans la → and and yin → represents are retained with local clause and copula evidence; the initial realm of capture is tightened to desire.
All original spans have explicit dispositions with their original IDs, depths, texts and ranges. Every original omission has a disposition. Full final rationales, selected occurrences and unwrapped ranges are in final-decisions.json.
Counts: 42 spans; 26 non-null d5; 4 d7; 0 nulls; 0 errata. 80 original span dispositions; 4 final material omissions.
Canonical final generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=6122 bytes. Exact actual native streams: ../native/final-319-generator-v1/.
Spec SHA256 5486fc337b30028773a08ed6540f8d5a3ea208cc4e9814068611fa8d80b12144; 6246 bytes.
Body SHA256 4504d0355b0abb7baa813911c2a46b87d97989e28096c23cdb708cb2f02ec718; 6122 bytes.
The independent canonical resolver exits 0, empty stderr, and equals all intended ranges. Complete rendered Wylie and English equal the original spine fields.
Six exact successful original generator executions were reused after all source, dependency, stdin, stdout/body, stderr and exit identities were checked; all six originals were independently resolved. Each reconciled final generator was executed once.
Every original SQL/external-corpus count and full record was independently rechecked; indexed full master objects and spine entries are preserved, not promoted. Copy/pin proofs and complete material sources are in ../evidence/.
Ten original category suspicions each received all five refutation grounds in errata-audit.json. No independent publication witness or new source correction is established; register remains 194.
No d5/d7 supplied-head allowance is proposed. GMR wording remains verbatim and machine matches provisional.
The 321 physical source spans CRLF lines; explicit complete-byte-extent whitespace folding is verified. Original failed contiguity assumptions remain frozen under ../originals/.
Mechanical checks do not establish semantic truth; the independent skeptical pass and root acceptance remain required.

===== tibetan/320/report.md =====
# C05:320 — independent Tibetan-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3a (through 312); no future through-318 acceptance claimed.
Challenge the division of shared negation first: the full negative-ripening phrase is proposed, and both color negatives remain distributed omissions. The two causative pieces are retained because their full English is discontinuous.
Counts: 11 spans; 0 nulls; 7 non-null d5 word pairs (7 total d5); 2 d7 members.
Canonical generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=2176 bytes. Exact native output is native/generator-v1/stdout.bin.
Canonical resolver EXIT=0; every intended source/English occurrence range matches resolved-ranges.json. Both rendered full fields equal the original source.
Spec SHA256 1251d32bd5d8c9e8ca42d72ec0acf6108c6f92db7c18a9662643e354a4a113d3; body SHA256 784770e6d152b28d01e27fad514119c46bf4e65e9635bc47dbdc85619923601a.
Full source and physical evidence: ../evidence/full-original-spine-context.json and physical-complete-row-proof.json. Source bytes are unmodified.
Full master objects and exact counts: ../evidence/full-lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-full-lexical-evidence.json. Comparanda remain reference only.
Every complete-field and leading-phrase parallel query returns this row alone; all-course results and C16/P1/TCS scope are preserved in ../evidence/.
Errata: 0. All five category screens with all five refutation grounds are recorded in errata-audit.json; uncertain suspicions are not filed.
Material omissions and all occurrence-specific rationales are in decisions.json. No uncertain correspondence is represented as null.
All alignments remain provisional machine proposals; mechanical passes do not assert final semantic approval.

===== english/320/report.md =====
# C05:320 — English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3af9cbb47599bb40286637fc9e683b7cdf (through 312); future landing baseline was not supplied or claimed.
Challenge consolidated negation first: the first two mi stay omitted rather than being assigned to either/or or falsely nulled; the negative ripening phrase owns never ripen.
Quoted it and explanatory black are selected before the heading. The discontinuous causative is split into byed pa/brings and zad/end without claiming its intervening object.
Coverage: 9 spans; 8 non-null d5; 8 total d5; 0 d7; 0 nulls; 0 new errata.
All source-specific decisions, exact ranges and explicit omissions: decisions.json. Full source/context, material corpus/master objects and original physical bytes: ../evidence/.
Five-ground category screens: errata-audit.json. Exact-field searches each return this row alone; broader context queries are recorded with full rows and original corpus objects. No publication independence is asserted.
Canonical generator and resolver each ran once, both exit 0 and empty stderr. Full native streams, argv and hashes are under native-*-v1/.
EXIT=0; body bytes=2036; body SHA256=636cae169612eaee8b5dbda11c0bef9b0cc836e0762716575811c77868056d8b.
Spec SHA256=85ff3ec1d3ccc45acf96ad4bb0a53a0133900313cd021fe5693b177901af1101. Canonical ranges match all intended occurrences; rendered complete source strings equal the originals.
Mechanical checks are not semantic approval. Machine matches remain provisional and are never promoted into hgm_gloss.
One evidence-collection assumption failed before lexical queries: the verse ACIP is line-wrapped in its physical file. The failed method is preserved; corrected proof uses reversible Latin-1 whitespace folding and a complete raw extent.

===== tibetan/321/report.md =====
# C05:321 — independent Tibetan-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3a (through 312); no future through-318 acceptance claimed.
Challenge chos bzod → Phenomena, mastery and the second whole path → uninterrupted first. Both are complete technical expressions with contextual/full-master support; the mined chos bzod gloss is explicitly not relied on.
Counts: 32 spans; 0 nulls; 22 non-null d5 word pairs (22 total d5); 8 d7 members.
Canonical generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=4751 bytes. Exact native output is native/generator-v1/stdout.bin.
Canonical resolver EXIT=0; every intended source/English occurrence range matches resolved-ranges.json. Both rendered full fields equal the original source.
Spec SHA256 70f43e127bc7ccdf320f53d822f48bb65e2d3ab300c32fee2dd233d9f3c953cd; body SHA256 94e4a9e4cf7202e0fa360afa5da0578ecfdbb023c0f8d7824f80dc46d4219b4f.
Full source and physical evidence: ../evidence/full-original-spine-context.json and physical-complete-row-proof.json. Source bytes are unmodified.
Full master objects and exact counts: ../evidence/full-lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-full-lexical-evidence.json. Comparanda remain reference only.
Every complete-field and leading-phrase parallel query returns this row alone; all-course results and C16/P1/TCS scope are preserved in ../evidence/.
Errata: 0. All five category screens with all five refutation grounds are recorded in errata-audit.json; uncertain suspicions are not filed.
Material omissions and all occurrence-specific rationales are in decisions.json. No uncertain correspondence is represented as null.
All alignments remain provisional machine proposals; mechanical passes do not assert final semantic approval.

===== english/321/report.md =====
# C05:321 — English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. Source origin 49b66f3af9cbb47599bb40286637fc9e683b7cdf (through 312); future landing baseline was not supplied or claimed.
Challenge chos bzod first: the full verse and C05:322 explain Phenomena, mastery, while the original master stores a conflicting auto-mined claim. No hgm_gloss edit is proposed.
The second lam stays unbanked because the English has uninterrupted arising with no separate path noun. C05:324 is used only as context for concentration and final arising, not as authority for an unrelated emendation.
Coverage: 31 spans; 24 non-null d5; 24 total d5; 6 d7; 0 nulls; 0 new errata.
All source-specific decisions, exact ranges and explicit omissions: decisions.json. Full source/context, material corpus/master objects and original physical bytes: ../evidence/.
Five-ground category screens: errata-audit.json. Exact-field searches each return this row alone; broader context queries are recorded with full rows and original corpus objects. No publication independence is asserted.
Canonical generator and resolver each ran once, both exit 0 and empty stderr. Full native streams, argv and hashes are under native-*-v1/.
EXIT=0; body bytes=4676; body SHA256=0f6e8fdf1e1dc86239b5062fa3b74dcb68de7be564a3d38522de81b542e2fa75.
Spec SHA256=50c0e5991bc397a5e371116d70c31ba201defef92e6b63469ddec21c43b9f50a. Canonical ranges match all intended occurrences; rendered complete source strings equal the originals.
Mechanical checks are not semantic approval. Machine matches remain provisional and are never promoted into hgm_gloss.
One evidence-collection assumption failed before lexical queries: the verse ACIP is line-wrapped in its physical file. The failed method is preserved; corrected proof uses reversible Latin-1 whitespace folding and a complete raw extent.

===== reconciled/321/report.md =====
# C05:321 — independent reconciliation
Producer: Codex. Source/governance origin 49b66f3a through 312; a later through-318 acceptance baseline was not supplied or consumed.
Retain the full chos bzod compound and two members despite the conflicting original auto-mined master gloss; C05:322 and the expanded full HGM term support the source match. Restore whole chags bral ba with two members and the second whole bar chad med lam → uninterrupted; no lam → arising or degenerate member.
All original spans have explicit dispositions with their original IDs, depths, texts and ranges. Every original omission has a disposition. Full final rationales, selected occurrences and unwrapped ranges are in final-decisions.json.
Counts: 32 spans; 22 non-null d5; 8 d7; 0 nulls; 0 errata. 63 original span dispositions; 5 final material omissions.
Canonical final generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5082 bytes. Exact actual native streams: ../native/final-321-generator-v1/.
Spec SHA256 a377a5ca1882ec6d08d936a40cf8941fc79ae27fe898c45f8d6569ca0a73559a; 5204 bytes.
Body SHA256 d77f67c1652f4b081a9e8e9056af906a362f7911a48dfd86f2173a625d2c9ac3; 5082 bytes.
The independent canonical resolver exits 0, empty stderr, and equals all intended ranges. Complete rendered Wylie and English equal the original spine fields.
Six exact successful original generator executions were reused after all source, dependency, stdin, stdout/body, stderr and exit identities were checked; all six originals were independently resolved. Each reconciled final generator was executed once.
Every original SQL/external-corpus count and full record was independently rechecked; indexed full master objects and spine entries are preserved, not promoted. Copy/pin proofs and complete material sources are in ../evidence/.
Ten original category suspicions each received all five refutation grounds in errata-audit.json. No independent publication witness or new source correction is established; register remains 194.
No d5/d7 supplied-head allowance is proposed. GMR wording remains verbatim and machine matches provisional.
The 321 physical source spans CRLF lines; explicit complete-byte-extent whitespace folding is verified. Original failed contiguity assumptions remain frozen under ../originals/.
Mechanical checks do not establish semantic truth; the independent skeptical pass and root acceptance remain required.
